from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def azimint_hist(data: Float, radius: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], npt: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    histu = np.histogram(radius, npt)[0]
    histw = np.histogram(radius, npt, weights=data)[0]
    return histw / histu

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, domain-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda data, radius, npt: npt != 0)
def azimint_naive(data: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], radius: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], npt: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    rmax = radius.max()
    res = np.zeros(npt, dtype=np.float64)
    for i in range(npt):
        r1 = rmax * i / npt
        r2 = rmax * (i + 1) / npt
        mask_r12 = np.logical_and(r1 <= radius, radius < r2)
        values_r12 = data[mask_r12]
        res[i] = values_r12.mean()
    return res

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def build_up_b(b: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], rho: Float, dt: Float, u: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], v: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], dx: Float, dy: Float):
    b[1:-1, 1:-1] = rho * (1 / dt * ((u[1:-1, 2:] - u[1:-1, 0:-2]) / (2 * dx) + (v[2:, 1:-1] - v[0:-2, 1:-1]) / (2 * dy)) - ((u[1:-1, 2:] - u[1:-1, 0:-2]) / (2 * dx)) ** 2 - 2 * ((u[2:, 1:-1] - u[0:-2, 1:-1]) / (2 * dy) * (v[1:-1, 2:] - v[1:-1, 0:-2]) / (2 * dx)) - ((v[2:, 1:-1] - v[0:-2, 1:-1]) / (2 * dy)) ** 2)

@zk_chip
def pressure_poisson(nit: Integer, p: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], dx: Float, dy: Float, b: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    pn = np.empty_like(p)
    pn = p.copy()
    for q in range(nit):
        pn = p.copy()
        p[1:-1, 1:-1] = ((pn[1:-1, 2:] + pn[1:-1, 0:-2]) * dy ** 2 + (pn[2:, 1:-1] + pn[0:-2, 1:-1]) * dx ** 2) / (2 * (dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)) - dx ** 2 * dy ** 2 / (2 * (dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)) * b[1:-1, 1:-1]
        p[:, -1] = p[:, -2]
        p[0, :] = p[1, :]
        p[:, 0] = p[:, 1]
        p[-1, :] = 0

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda nx, ny, nt, nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu: 0 <= nt <= 1024)
@requires(lambda nx, ny, nt, nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu: 0 <= nit <= 1024)
@requires(lambda nx, ny, nt, nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu: dt != 0.0)
@requires(lambda nx, ny, nt, nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu: dx != 0.0)
@requires(lambda nx, ny, nt, nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu: dy != 0.0)
def cavity_flow(nx: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], ny: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], nt: Integer, nit: Integer, u: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], v: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], dt: Float, dx: Float, dy: Float, p: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], rho: Float, nu: Float):
    un = np.empty_like(u)
    vn = np.empty_like(v)
    b = np.zeros((ny, nx))
    for n in range(nt):
        un = u.copy()
        vn = v.copy()
        build_up_b(b, rho, dt, u, v, dx, dy)
        pressure_poisson(nit, p, dx, dy, b)
        u[1:-1, 1:-1] = un[1:-1, 1:-1] - un[1:-1, 1:-1] * dt / dx * (un[1:-1, 1:-1] - un[1:-1, 0:-2]) - vn[1:-1, 1:-1] * dt / dy * (un[1:-1, 1:-1] - un[0:-2, 1:-1]) - dt / (2 * rho * dx) * (p[1:-1, 2:] - p[1:-1, 0:-2]) + nu * (dt / dx ** 2 * (un[1:-1, 2:] - 2 * un[1:-1, 1:-1] + un[1:-1, 0:-2]) + dt / dy ** 2 * (un[2:, 1:-1] - 2 * un[1:-1, 1:-1] + un[0:-2, 1:-1]))
        v[1:-1, 1:-1] = vn[1:-1, 1:-1] - un[1:-1, 1:-1] * dt / dx * (vn[1:-1, 1:-1] - vn[1:-1, 0:-2]) - vn[1:-1, 1:-1] * dt / dy * (vn[1:-1, 1:-1] - vn[0:-2, 1:-1]) - dt / (2 * rho * dy) * (p[2:, 1:-1] - p[0:-2, 1:-1]) + nu * (dt / dx ** 2 * (vn[1:-1, 2:] - 2 * vn[1:-1, 1:-1] + vn[1:-1, 0:-2]) + dt / dy ** 2 * (vn[2:, 1:-1] - 2 * vn[1:-1, 1:-1] + vn[0:-2, 1:-1]))
        u[0, :] = 0
        u[:, 0] = 0
        u[:, -1] = 0
        u[-1, :] = 1
        v[0, :] = 0
        v[-1, :] = 0
        v[:, 0] = 0
        v[:, -1] = 0

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: data-dependent-loop, denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add an explicit iteration cap or classify as fail-closed until a bound is available.
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: while loops require a public/padded maximum iteration count.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def build_up_b(rho: Float, dt: Float, dx: Float, dy: Float, u: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], v: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    b = np.zeros_like(u)
    b[1:-1, 1:-1] = rho * (1 / dt * ((u[1:-1, 2:] - u[1:-1, 0:-2]) / (2 * dx) + (v[2:, 1:-1] - v[0:-2, 1:-1]) / (2 * dy)) - ((u[1:-1, 2:] - u[1:-1, 0:-2]) / (2 * dx)) ** 2 - 2 * ((u[2:, 1:-1] - u[0:-2, 1:-1]) / (2 * dy) * (v[1:-1, 2:] - v[1:-1, 0:-2]) / (2 * dx)) - ((v[2:, 1:-1] - v[0:-2, 1:-1]) / (2 * dy)) ** 2)
    b[1:-1, -1] = rho * (1 / dt * ((u[1:-1, 0] - u[1:-1, -2]) / (2 * dx) + (v[2:, -1] - v[0:-2, -1]) / (2 * dy)) - ((u[1:-1, 0] - u[1:-1, -2]) / (2 * dx)) ** 2 - 2 * ((u[2:, -1] - u[0:-2, -1]) / (2 * dy) * (v[1:-1, 0] - v[1:-1, -2]) / (2 * dx)) - ((v[2:, -1] - v[0:-2, -1]) / (2 * dy)) ** 2)
    b[1:-1, 0] = rho * (1 / dt * ((u[1:-1, 1] - u[1:-1, -1]) / (2 * dx) + (v[2:, 0] - v[0:-2, 0]) / (2 * dy)) - ((u[1:-1, 1] - u[1:-1, -1]) / (2 * dx)) ** 2 - 2 * ((u[2:, 0] - u[0:-2, 0]) / (2 * dy) * (v[1:-1, 1] - v[1:-1, -1]) / (2 * dx)) - ((v[2:, 0] - v[0:-2, 0]) / (2 * dy)) ** 2)
    return b

@zk_chip
def pressure_poisson_periodic(nit: Integer, p: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], dx: Float, dy: Float, b: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    pn = np.empty_like(p)
    for q in range(nit):
        pn = p.copy()
        p[1:-1, 1:-1] = ((pn[1:-1, 2:] + pn[1:-1, 0:-2]) * dy ** 2 + (pn[2:, 1:-1] + pn[0:-2, 1:-1]) * dx ** 2) / (2 * (dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)) - dx ** 2 * dy ** 2 / (2 * (dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)) * b[1:-1, 1:-1]
        p[1:-1, -1] = ((pn[1:-1, 0] + pn[1:-1, -2]) * dy ** 2 + (pn[2:, -1] + pn[0:-2, -1]) * dx ** 2) / (2 * (dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)) - dx ** 2 * dy ** 2 / (2 * (dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)) * b[1:-1, -1]
        p[1:-1, 0] = ((pn[1:-1, 1] + pn[1:-1, -1]) * dy ** 2 + (pn[2:, 0] + pn[0:-2, 0]) * dx ** 2) / (2 * (dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)) - dx ** 2 * dy ** 2 / (2 * (dx ** 2 + dy ** 2)) * b[1:-1, 0]
        p[-1, :] = p[-2, :]
        p[0, :] = p[1, :]

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu, F: 0 <= nit <= 1024)
@requires(lambda nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu, F: dt != 0.0)
@requires(lambda nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu, F: dx != 0.0)
@requires(lambda nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu, F: dy != 0.0)
@requires(lambda nit, u, v, dt, dx, dy, p, rho, nu, F: rho != 0.0)
def channel_flow(nit: Integer, u: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], v: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], dt: Float, dx: Float, dy: Float, p: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], rho: Float, nu: Float, F: Float):
    udiff = 1
    stepcount = 0
    while udiff > 0.001:
        un = u.copy()
        vn = v.copy()
        b = build_up_b(rho, dt, dx, dy, u, v)
        pressure_poisson_periodic(nit, p, dx, dy, b)
        u[1:-1, 1:-1] = un[1:-1, 1:-1] - un[1:-1, 1:-1] * dt / dx * (un[1:-1, 1:-1] - un[1:-1, 0:-2]) - vn[1:-1, 1:-1] * dt / dy * (un[1:-1, 1:-1] - un[0:-2, 1:-1]) - dt / (2 * rho * dx) * (p[1:-1, 2:] - p[1:-1, 0:-2]) + nu * (dt / dx ** 2 * (un[1:-1, 2:] - 2 * un[1:-1, 1:-1] + un[1:-1, 0:-2]) + dt / dy ** 2 * (un[2:, 1:-1] - 2 * un[1:-1, 1:-1] + un[0:-2, 1:-1])) + F * dt
        v[1:-1, 1:-1] = vn[1:-1, 1:-1] - un[1:-1, 1:-1] * dt / dx * (vn[1:-1, 1:-1] - vn[1:-1, 0:-2]) - vn[1:-1, 1:-1] * dt / dy * (vn[1:-1, 1:-1] - vn[0:-2, 1:-1]) - dt / (2 * rho * dy) * (p[2:, 1:-1] - p[0:-2, 1:-1]) + nu * (dt / dx ** 2 * (vn[1:-1, 2:] - 2 * vn[1:-1, 1:-1] + vn[1:-1, 0:-2]) + dt / dy ** 2 * (vn[2:, 1:-1] - 2 * vn[1:-1, 1:-1] + vn[0:-2, 1:-1]))
        u[1:-1, -1] = un[1:-1, -1] - un[1:-1, -1] * dt / dx * (un[1:-1, -1] - un[1:-1, -2]) - vn[1:-1, -1] * dt / dy * (un[1:-1, -1] - un[0:-2, -1]) - dt / (2 * rho * dx) * (p[1:-1, 0] - p[1:-1, -2]) + nu * (dt / dx ** 2 * (un[1:-1, 0] - 2 * un[1:-1, -1] + un[1:-1, -2]) + dt / dy ** 2 * (un[2:, -1] - 2 * un[1:-1, -1] + un[0:-2, -1])) + F * dt
        u[1:-1, 0] = un[1:-1, 0] - un[1:-1, 0] * dt / dx * (un[1:-1, 0] - un[1:-1, -1]) - vn[1:-1, 0] * dt / dy * (un[1:-1, 0] - un[0:-2, 0]) - dt / (2 * rho * dx) * (p[1:-1, 1] - p[1:-1, -1]) + nu * (dt / dx ** 2 * (un[1:-1, 1] - 2 * un[1:-1, 0] + un[1:-1, -1]) + dt / dy ** 2 * (un[2:, 0] - 2 * un[1:-1, 0] + un[0:-2, 0])) + F * dt
        v[1:-1, -1] = vn[1:-1, -1] - un[1:-1, -1] * dt / dx * (vn[1:-1, -1] - vn[1:-1, -2]) - vn[1:-1, -1] * dt / dy * (vn[1:-1, -1] - vn[0:-2, -1]) - dt / (2 * rho * dy) * (p[2:, -1] - p[0:-2, -1]) + nu * (dt / dx ** 2 * (vn[1:-1, 0] - 2 * vn[1:-1, -1] + vn[1:-1, -2]) + dt / dy ** 2 * (vn[2:, -1] - 2 * vn[1:-1, -1] + vn[0:-2, -1]))
        v[1:-1, 0] = vn[1:-1, 0] - un[1:-1, 0] * dt / dx * (vn[1:-1, 0] - vn[1:-1, -1]) - vn[1:-1, 0] * dt / dy * (vn[1:-1, 0] - vn[0:-2, 0]) - dt / (2 * rho * dy) * (p[2:, 0] - p[0:-2, 0]) + nu * (dt / dx ** 2 * (vn[1:-1, 1] - 2 * vn[1:-1, 0] + vn[1:-1, -1]) + dt / dy ** 2 * (vn[2:, 0] - 2 * vn[1:-1, 0] + vn[0:-2, 0]))
        u[0, :] = 0
        u[-1, :] = 0
        v[0, :] = 0
        v[-1, :] = 0
        udiff = (np.sum(u) - np.sum(un)) / np.sum(u)
        stepcount += 1
    return stepcount

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: complex, denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, linalg, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Record square/invertible/SPD requirements where the linalg call needs them.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: Complex kernels require Complex dtype support or a fail-closed classification.
# Diagnostic: np.linalg kernels may stay fail-closed until support is selected.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def contour_integral(NR: Integer, NM: Integer, slab_per_bc: Integer, Ham: Complex[Array, 'S<=1024 NR<=1024 NR<=1024'], int_pts: Complex[Array, 'Z<=1024'], Y: Complex[Array, 'NR<=1024 NM<=1024']):
    P0 = np.zeros((NR, NM), dtype=np.complex128)
    P1 = np.zeros((NR, NM), dtype=np.complex128)
    for z in int_pts:
        Tz = np.zeros((NR, NR), dtype=np.complex128)
        for n in range(slab_per_bc + 1):
            zz = np.power(z, slab_per_bc / 2 - n)
            Tz += zz * Ham[n]
        if NR == NM:
            X = np.linalg.inv(Tz)
        else:
            X = np.linalg.solve(Tz, Y)
        if abs(z) < 1.0:
            X = -X
        P0 += X
        P1 += z * X
    return (P0, P1)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def crc16(data: Float, poly: Float=33800):
    """
    CRC-16-CCITT Algorithm
    """
    crc = 65535
    for b in data:
        cur_byte = 255 & b
        for _ in range(0, 8):
            if crc & 1 ^ cur_byte & 1:
                crc = crc >> 1 ^ poly
            else:
                crc >>= 1
            cur_byte >>= 1
    crc = ~crc & 65535
    crc = crc << 8 | crc >> 8 & 255
    return crc & 65535

# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def conv2d(input: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 H<=1024 W<=1024 C<=256'], weights: Float[Array, 'K<=256 K<=256 C<=256 F<=256']):
    K = weights.shape[0]
    N = input.shape[0]
    H_out = input.shape[1] - K + 1
    W_out = input.shape[2] - K + 1
    C_out = weights.shape[3]
    output = np.empty((N, H_out, W_out, C_out), dtype=np.float32)
    for i in range(H_out):
        for j in range(W_out):
            output[:, i, j, :] = np.sum(input[:, i:i + K, j:j + K, :, np.newaxis] * weights[np.newaxis, :, :, :], axis=(1, 2, 3))
    return output

@zk_circuit
def conv2d_bias(input: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 H<=1024 W<=1024 C<=256'], weights: Float[Array, 'K<=256 K<=256 C<=256 F<=256'], bias: Float[Array, 'F<=256']):
    return conv2d(input, weights) + bias

from zinnia import *
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def relu(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    return np.maximum(x, 0)

@zk_chip
def conv2d(input: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024 L<=256'], weights: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024']):
    K = weights.shape[0]
    N = input.shape[0]
    H_out = input.shape[1] - K + 1
    W_out = input.shape[2] - K + 1
    C_out = weights.shape[3]
    output = np.empty((N, H_out, W_out, C_out), dtype=np.float32)
    for i in range(H_out):
        for j in range(W_out):
            output[:, i, j, :] = np.sum(input[:, i:i + K, j:j + K, :, np.newaxis] * weights[np.newaxis, :, :, :], axis=(1, 2, 3))
    return output

@zk_chip
def maxpool2d(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024 L<=256']):
    output = np.empty([x.shape[0], x.shape[1] // 2, x.shape[2] // 2, x.shape[3]], dtype=x.dtype)
    for i in range(x.shape[1] // 2):
        for j in range(x.shape[2] // 2):
            output[:, i, j, :] = np.max(x[:, 2 * i:2 * i + 2, 2 * j:2 * j + 2, :], axis=(1, 2))
    return output

@zk_circuit
def lenet5(input: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 H<=1024 W<=1024 C<=256'], conv1: Float[Array, 'K<=256 K<=256 C<=256 F1<=256'], conv1bias: Float[Array, 'F1<=256'], conv2: Float[Array, 'K2<=256 K2<=256 F1<=256 F2<=256'], conv2bias: Float[Array, 'F2<=256'], fc1w: Float[Array, 'C0<=1024 C1<=1024'], fc1b: Float[Array, 'C1<=1024'], fc2w: Float[Array, 'C1<=1024 C2<=1024'], fc2b: Float[Array, 'C2<=1024'], fc3w: Float[Array, 'C2<=1024 C3<=1024'], fc3b: Float[Array, 'C3<=1024'], N: Integer, C_before_fc1: Integer):
    x = relu(conv2d(input, conv1) + conv1bias)
    x = maxpool2d(x)
    x = relu(conv2d(x, conv2) + conv2bias)
    x = maxpool2d(x)
    x = np.reshape(x, (N, C_before_fc1))
    x = relu(x @ fc1w + fc1b)
    x = relu(x @ fc2w + fc2b)
    return x @ fc3w + fc3b

from zinnia import *
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def relu(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    return np.maximum(x, 0)

@zk_chip
def softmax(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    tmp_max = np.max(x, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    tmp_out = np.exp(x - tmp_max)
    tmp_sum = np.sum(tmp_out, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    return tmp_out / tmp_sum

@zk_circuit
def mlp(input: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], w1: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 H<=1024'], b1: Float, w2: Float[Array, 'H<=1024 K<=1024'], b2: Float, w3: Float[Array, 'K<=1024 M<=1024'], b3: Float):
    x = relu(input @ w1 + b1)
    x = relu(x @ w2 + b2)
    x = softmax(x @ w3 + b3)
    return x

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, domain-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def relu(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    return np.maximum(x, 0)

@zk_chip
def conv2d(input: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024 L<=256'], weights: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024']):
    K = weights.shape[0]
    N = input.shape[0]
    H_out = input.shape[1] - K + 1
    W_out = input.shape[2] - K + 1
    C_out = weights.shape[3]
    output = np.empty((N, H_out, W_out, C_out), dtype=np.float32)
    for i in range(H_out):
        for j in range(W_out):
            output[:, i, j, :] = np.sum(input[:, i:i + K, j:j + K, :, np.newaxis] * weights[np.newaxis, :, :, :], axis=(1, 2, 3))
    return output

@zk_chip
def batchnorm2d(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], eps: Float=1e-05):
    mean = np.mean(x, axis=0, keepdims=True)
    std = np.std(x, axis=0, keepdims=True)
    return (x - mean) / np.sqrt(std + eps)

@zk_circuit
def resnet_basicblock(input: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 H<=1024 W<=1024 C<=256'], conv1: Float[Array, 'K1<=256 K1<=256 C<=256 F1<=256'], conv2: Float[Array, 'K2<=256 K2<=256 F1<=256 F2<=256'], conv3: Float[Array, 'K3<=256 K3<=256 F2<=256 C<=256']):
    padded = np.zeros((input.shape[0], input.shape[1] + 2, input.shape[2] + 2, conv1.shape[3]))
    padded[:, 1:-1, 1:-1, :] = conv2d(input, conv1)
    x = batchnorm2d(padded)
    x = relu(x)
    x = conv2d(x, conv2)
    x = batchnorm2d(x)
    x = relu(x)
    x = conv2d(x, conv3)
    x = batchnorm2d(x)
    return relu(x + input)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.

import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def go_fast(a: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    trace = 0.0
    for i in range(a.shape[0]):
        trace += np.tanh(a[i, i])
    return a + trace

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: complex, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: Complex kernels require Complex dtype support or a fail-closed classification.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def mandelbrot(xmin: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], xmax: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], ymin: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], ymax: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], xn: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], yn: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], maxiter: Float, horizon: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024']=2.0):
    X = np.linspace(xmin, xmax, xn, dtype=np.float64)
    Y = np.linspace(ymin, ymax, yn, dtype=np.float64)
    C = X + Y[:, None] * 1j
    N = np.zeros(C.shape, dtype=np.int64)
    Z = np.zeros(C.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
    for n in range(maxiter):
        I = np.less(abs(Z), horizon)
        N[I] = n
        Z[I] = Z[I] ** 2 + C[I]
    N[N == maxiter - 1] = 0
    return (Z, N)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: complex, domain-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: Complex kernels require Complex dtype support or a fail-closed classification.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax, xn, yn, itermax, horizon: 0 <= itermax <= 1024)
def mandelbrot(xmin: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], xmax: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], ymin: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], ymax: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], xn: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], yn: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], itermax: Integer, horizon: Float=2.0):
    Xi, Yi = np.mgrid[0:xn, 0:yn]
    X = np.linspace(xmin, xmax, xn, dtype=np.float64)[Xi]
    Y = np.linspace(ymin, ymax, yn, dtype=np.float64)[Yi]
    C = X + Y * 1j
    N_ = np.zeros(C.shape, dtype=np.int64)
    Z_ = np.zeros(C.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
    Xi.shape = Yi.shape = C.shape = xn * yn
    Z = np.zeros(C.shape, np.complex128)
    for i in range(itermax):
        if not len(Z):
            break
        np.multiply(Z, Z, Z)
        np.add(Z, C, Z)
        I = abs(Z) > horizon
        N_[Xi[I], Yi[I]] = i + 1
        Z_[Xi[I], Yi[I]] = Z[I]
        np.logical_not(I, I)
        Z = Z[I]
        Xi, Yi = (Xi[I], Yi[I])
        C = C[I]
    return (Z_.T, N_.T)

from zinnia import *
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def getAcc(pos: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 C<=3'], mass: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], G: Float, softening: Float):
    """
    Calculate the acceleration on each particle due to Newton's Law 
    pos  is an N x 3 matrix of positions
    mass is an N x 1 vector of masses
    G is Newton's Gravitational constant
    softening is the softening length
    a is N x 3 matrix of accelerations
    """
    x = pos[:, 0:1]
    y = pos[:, 1:2]
    z = pos[:, 2:3]
    dx = x.T - x
    dy = y.T - y
    dz = z.T - z
    inv_r3 = dx ** 2 + dy ** 2 + dz ** 2 + softening ** 2
    inv_r3[inv_r3 > 0] = inv_r3[inv_r3 > 0] ** (-1.5)
    ax = G * (dx * inv_r3) @ mass
    ay = G * (dy * inv_r3) @ mass
    az = G * (dz * inv_r3) @ mass
    a = np.hstack((ax, ay, az))
    return a

@zk_chip
def getEnergy(pos: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 C<=3'], vel: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 C<=3'], mass: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], G: Float):
    """
    Get kinetic energy (KE) and potential energy (PE) of simulation
    pos is N x 3 matrix of positions
    vel is N x 3 matrix of velocities
    mass is an N x 1 vector of masses
    G is Newton's Gravitational constant
    KE is the kinetic energy of the system
    PE is the potential energy of the system
    """
    KE = 0.5 * np.sum(mass * vel ** 2)
    x = pos[:, 0:1]
    y = pos[:, 1:2]
    z = pos[:, 2:3]
    dx = x.T - x
    dy = y.T - y
    dz = z.T - z
    inv_r = np.sqrt(dx ** 2 + dy ** 2 + dz ** 2)
    inv_r[inv_r > 0] = 1.0 / inv_r[inv_r > 0]
    PE = G * np.sum(np.triu(-(mass * mass.T) * inv_r, 1))
    return (KE, PE)

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda mass, pos, vel, N, Nt, dt, G, softening: 0 <= N <= 1024)
@requires(lambda mass, pos, vel, N, Nt, dt, G, softening: dt != 0.0)
def nbody(mass: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], pos: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 C<=3'], vel: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 C<=3'], N: Integer, Nt: Integer, dt: Float, G: Float, softening: Float):
    vel -= np.mean(mass * vel, axis=0) / np.mean(mass)
    acc = getAcc(pos, mass, G, softening)
    KE = np.ndarray(Nt + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    PE = np.ndarray(Nt + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    KE[0], PE[0] = getEnergy(pos, vel, mass, G)
    t = 0.0
    for i in range(Nt):
        vel += acc * dt / 2.0
        pos += vel * dt
        acc = getAcc(pos, mass, G, softening)
        vel += acc * dt / 2.0
        t += dt
        KE[i + 1], PE[i + 1] = getEnergy(pos, vel, mass, G)
    return (KE, PE)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda TSTEPS, N, u: 0 <= N <= 1024)
@requires(lambda TSTEPS, N, u: N != 0)
@requires(lambda TSTEPS, N, u: TSTEPS != 0.0)
def kernel(TSTEPS: Float, N: Integer, u: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    v = np.empty(u.shape, dtype=u.dtype)
    p = np.empty(u.shape, dtype=u.dtype)
    q = np.empty(u.shape, dtype=u.dtype)
    DX = 1.0 / N
    DY = 1.0 / N
    DT = 1.0 / TSTEPS
    B1 = 2.0
    B2 = 1.0
    mul1 = B1 * DT / (DX * DX)
    mul2 = B2 * DT / (DY * DY)
    a = -mul1 / 2.0
    b = 1.0 + mul2
    c = a
    d = -mul2 / 2.0
    e = 1.0 + mul2
    f = d
    for t in range(1, TSTEPS + 1):
        v[0, 1:N - 1] = 1.0
        p[1:N - 1, 0] = 0.0
        q[1:N - 1, 0] = v[0, 1:N - 1]
        for j in range(1, N - 1):
            p[1:N - 1, j] = -c / (a * p[1:N - 1, j - 1] + b)
            q[1:N - 1, j] = (-d * u[j, 0:N - 2] + (1.0 + 2.0 * d) * u[j, 1:N - 1] - f * u[j, 2:N] - a * q[1:N - 1, j - 1]) / (a * p[1:N - 1, j - 1] + b)
        v[N - 1, 1:N - 1] = 1.0
        for j in range(N - 2, 0, -1):
            v[j, 1:N - 1] = p[1:N - 1, j] * v[j + 1, 1:N - 1] + q[1:N - 1, j]
        u[1:N - 1, 0] = 1.0
        p[1:N - 1, 0] = 0.0
        q[1:N - 1, 0] = u[1:N - 1, 0]
        for j in range(1, N - 1):
            p[1:N - 1, j] = -f / (d * p[1:N - 1, j - 1] + e)
            q[1:N - 1, j] = (-a * v[0:N - 2, j] + (1.0 + 2.0 * a) * v[1:N - 1, j] - c * v[2:N, j] - d * q[1:N - 1, j - 1]) / (d * p[1:N - 1, j - 1] + e)
        u[1:N - 1, N - 1] = 1.0
        for j in range(N - 2, 0, -1):
            u[1:N - 1, j] = p[1:N - 1, j] * u[1:N - 1, j + 1] + q[1:N - 1, j]
    return u

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: domain-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(A: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 N<=1024']):
    A[0, 0] = np.sqrt(A[0, 0])
    for i in range(1, A.shape[0]):
        for j in range(i):
            A[i, j] -= np.dot(A[i, :j], A[j, :j])
            A[i, j] /= A[j, j]
        A[i, i] -= np.dot(A[i, :i], A[i, :i])
        A[i, i] = np.sqrt(A[i, i])

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: domain-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(M: Integer, float_n: Float, data: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    mean = np.mean(data, axis=0)
    stddev = np.std(data, axis=0)
    stddev[stddev <= 0.1] = 1.0
    data -= mean
    data /= np.sqrt(float_n) * stddev
    corr = np.eye(M, dtype=data.dtype)
    for i in range(M - 1):
        corr[i + 1:M, i] = corr[i, i + 1:M] = data[:, i] @ data[:, i + 1:M]
    return corr

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(M: Integer, float_n: Float, data: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    mean = np.mean(data, axis=0)
    data -= mean
    cov = np.zeros((M, M), dtype=data.dtype)
    for i in range(M):
        cov[i:M, i] = cov[i, i:M] = data[:, i] @ data[:, i:M] / (float_n - 1.0)
    return cov

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(alpha: Float, imgIn: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    k = (1.0 - np.exp(-alpha)) * (1.0 - np.exp(-alpha)) / (1.0 + alpha * np.exp(-alpha) - np.exp(2.0 * alpha))
    a1 = a5 = k
    a2 = a6 = k * np.exp(-alpha) * (alpha - 1.0)
    a3 = a7 = k * np.exp(-alpha) * (alpha + 1.0)
    a4 = a8 = -k * np.exp(-2.0 * alpha)
    b1 = 2.0 ** (-alpha)
    b2 = -np.exp(-2.0 * alpha)
    c1 = c2 = 1
    y1 = np.empty_like(imgIn)
    y1[:, 0] = a1 * imgIn[:, 0]
    y1[:, 1] = a1 * imgIn[:, 1] + a2 * imgIn[:, 0] + b1 * y1[:, 0]
    for j in range(2, imgIn.shape[1]):
        y1[:, j] = a1 * imgIn[:, j] + a2 * imgIn[:, j - 1] + b1 * y1[:, j - 1] + b2 * y1[:, j - 2]
    y2 = np.empty_like(imgIn)
    y2[:, -1] = 0.0
    y2[:, -2] = a3 * imgIn[:, -1]
    for j in range(imgIn.shape[1] - 3, -1, -1):
        y2[:, j] = a3 * imgIn[:, j + 1] + a4 * imgIn[:, j + 2] + b1 * y2[:, j + 1] + b2 * y2[:, j + 2]
    imgOut = c1 * (y1 + y2)
    y1[0, :] = a5 * imgOut[0, :]
    y1[1, :] = a5 * imgOut[1, :] + a6 * imgOut[0, :] + b1 * y1[0, :]
    for i in range(2, imgIn.shape[0]):
        y1[i, :] = a5 * imgOut[i, :] + a6 * imgOut[i - 1, :] + b1 * y1[i - 1, :] + b2 * y1[i - 2, :]
    y2[-1, :] = 0.0
    y2[-2, :] = a7 * imgOut[-1, :]
    for i in range(imgIn.shape[0] - 3, -1, -1):
        y2[i, :] = a7 * imgOut[i + 1, :] + a8 * imgOut[i + 2, :] + b1 * y2[i + 1, :] + b2 * y2[i + 2, :]
    imgOut[:] = c2 * (y1 + y2)
    return imgOut

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(r: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    y = np.empty_like(r)
    alpha = -r[0]
    beta = 1.0
    y[0] = -r[0]
    for k in range(1, r.shape[0]):
        beta *= 1.0 - alpha * alpha
        alpha = -(r[k] + np.dot(np.flip(r[:k]), y[:k])) / beta
        y[:k] += alpha * np.flip(y[:k])
        y[k] = alpha
    return y

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(TMAX: Float, ex: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], ey: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], hz: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], _fict_: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    for t in range(TMAX):
        ey[0, :] = _fict_[t]
        ey[1:, :] -= 0.5 * (hz[1:, :] - hz[:-1, :])
        ex[:, 1:] -= 0.5 * (hz[:, 1:] - hz[:, :-1])
        hz[:-1, :-1] -= 0.7 * (ex[:-1, 1:] - ex[:-1, :-1] + ey[1:, :-1] - ey[:-1, :-1])

import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(A: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    Q = np.zeros_like(A)
    R = np.zeros((A.shape[1], A.shape[1]), dtype=A.dtype)
    for k in range(A.shape[1]):
        nrm = np.dot(A[:, k], A[:, k])
        R[k, k] = np.sqrt(nrm)
        Q[:, k] = A[:, k] / R[k, k]
        for j in range(k + 1, A.shape[1]):
            R[k, j] = np.dot(Q[:, k], A[:, j])
            A[:, j] -= Q[:, k] * R[k, j]
    return (Q, R)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(A: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], b: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    x = np.zeros_like(b)
    y = np.zeros_like(b)
    for i in range(A.shape[0]):
        for j in range(i):
            A[i, j] -= A[i, :j] @ A[:j, j]
            A[i, j] /= A[j, j]
        for j in range(i, A.shape[0]):
            A[i, j] -= A[i, :i] @ A[:i, j]
    for i in range(A.shape[0]):
        y[i] = b[i] - A[i, :i] @ y[:i]
    for i in range(A.shape[0] - 1, -1, -1):
        x[i] = (y[i] - A[i, i + 1:] @ x[i + 1:]) / A[i, i]
    return (x, y)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def match(b1: Float, b2: Float):
    if b1 + b2 == 3:
        return 1
    else:
        return 0

@zk_circuit
def kernel(N: Integer, seq: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    table = np.zeros((N, N), np.int32)
    for i in range(N - 1, -1, -1):
        for j in range(i + 1, N):
            if j - 1 >= 0:
                table[i, j] = max(table[i, j], table[i, j - 1])
            if i + 1 < N:
                table[i, j] = max(table[i, j], table[i + 1, j])
            if j - 1 >= 0 and i + 1 < N:
                if i < j - 1:
                    table[i, j] = max(table[i, j], table[i + 1, j - 1] + match(seq[i], seq[j]))
                else:
                    table[i, j] = max(table[i, j], table[i + 1, j - 1])
            for k in range(i + 1, j):
                table[i, j] = max(table[i, j], table[i, k] + table[k + 1, j])
    return table

# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda TSTEPS, N, A: 0 <= N <= 1024)
def kernel(TSTEPS: Float, N: Integer, A: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    for t in range(0, TSTEPS - 1):
        for i in range(1, N - 1):
            A[i, 1:-1] += A[i - 1, :-2] + A[i - 1, 1:-1] + A[i - 1, 2:] + A[i, 2:] + A[i + 1, :-2] + A[i + 1, 1:-1] + A[i + 1, 2:]

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(alpha: Float, beta: Float, C: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], A: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], B: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    temp2 = np.empty((C.shape[1],), dtype=C.dtype)
    C *= beta
    for i in range(C.shape[0]):
        for j in range(C.shape[1]):
            C[:i, j] += alpha * B[i, j] * A[i, :i]
            temp2[j] = B[:i, j] @ A[i, :i]
        C[i, :] += alpha * B[i, :] * A[i, i] + alpha * temp2

# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate

import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def kernel(alpha: Float, beta: Float, C: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], A: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], B: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):

# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def arc_distance(theta_1: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], phi_1: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], theta_2: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], phi_2: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    """
    Calculates the pairwise arc distance between all points in vector a and b.
    """
    temp = np.sin((theta_2 - theta_1) / 2) ** 2 + np.cos(theta_1) * np.cos(theta_2) * np.sin((phi_2 - phi_1) / 2) ** 2
    distance_matrix = 2 * np.arctan2(np.sqrt(temp), np.sqrt(1 - temp))
    return distance_matrix

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def spmv(A_row: Integer[Array, 'N<=1024'], A_col: Integer[Array, 'N<=1024'], A_val: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    y = np.empty(A_row.size - 1, A_val.dtype)
    for i in range(A_row.size - 1):
        cols = A_col[A_row[i]:A_row[i + 1]]
        vals = A_val[A_row[i]:A_row[i + 1]]
        y[i] = vals @ x[cols]
    return y

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: complex, denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: Complex kernels require Complex dtype support or a fail-closed classification.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda N, R, K, x, y: R != 0)
def stockham_fft(N: Integer, R: Integer, K: Integer, x: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], y: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    i_coord, j_coord = np.mgrid[0:R, 0:R]
    dft_mat = np.empty((R, R), dtype=np.complex128)
    dft_mat = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * i_coord * j_coord / R)
    y[:] = x[:]
    ii_coord, jj_coord = np.mgrid[0:R, 0:R ** K]
    for i in range(K):
        yv = np.reshape(y, (R ** i, R, R ** (K - i - 1)))
        tmp_perm = np.transpose(yv, axes=(1, 0, 2))
        D = np.empty((R, R ** i, R ** (K - i - 1)), dtype=np.complex128)
        tmp = np.exp(-2j * np.pi * ii_coord[:, :R ** i] * jj_coord[:, :R ** i] / R ** (i + 1))
        D[:] = np.repeat(np.reshape(tmp, (R, R ** i, 1)), R ** (K - i - 1), axis=2)
        tmp_twid = np.reshape(tmp_perm, (N,)) * np.reshape(D, (N,))
        y[:] = np.reshape(dft_mat @ np.reshape(tmp_twid, (R, R ** (K - 1))), (N,))

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, mask-where
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Record mask shape equality and active-element bounds for where/nonzero-style indexing.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def hdiff(in_field: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], out_field: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], coeff: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024']):
    I, J, K = (out_field.shape[0], out_field.shape[1], out_field.shape[2])
    lap_field = 4.0 * in_field[1:I + 3, 1:J + 3, :] - (in_field[2:I + 4, 1:J + 3, :] + in_field[0:I + 2, 1:J + 3, :] + in_field[1:I + 3, 2:J + 4, :] + in_field[1:I + 3, 0:J + 2, :])
    res = lap_field[1:, 1:J + 1, :] - lap_field[:-1, 1:J + 1, :]
    flx_field = np.where(res * (in_field[2:I + 3, 2:J + 2, :] - in_field[1:I + 2, 2:J + 2, :]) > 0, 0, res)
    res = lap_field[1:I + 1, 1:, :] - lap_field[1:I + 1, :-1, :]
    fly_field = np.where(res * (in_field[2:I + 2, 2:J + 3, :] - in_field[2:I + 2, 1:J + 2, :]) > 0, 0, res)
    out_field[:, :, :] = in_field[2:I + 2, 2:J + 2, :] - coeff[:, :, :] * (flx_field[1:, :, :] - flx_field[:-1, :, :] + fly_field[:, 1:, :] - fly_field[:, :-1, :])

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np
BET_M = 0.5
BET_P = 0.5

@zk_circuit
def vadv(utens_stage: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], u_stage: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], wcon: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], u_pos: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], utens: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], dtr_stage: Float):
    I, J, K = (utens_stage.shape[0], utens_stage.shape[1], utens_stage.shape[2])
    ccol = np.ndarray((I, J, K), dtype=utens_stage.dtype)
    dcol = np.ndarray((I, J, K), dtype=utens_stage.dtype)
    data_col = np.ndarray((I, J), dtype=utens_stage.dtype)
    for k in range(1):
        gcv = 0.25 * (wcon[1:, :, k + 1] + wcon[:-1, :, k + 1])
        cs = gcv * BET_M
        ccol[:, :, k] = gcv * BET_P
        bcol = dtr_stage - ccol[:, :, k]
        correction_term = -cs * (u_stage[:, :, k + 1] - u_stage[:, :, k])
        dcol[:, :, k] = dtr_stage * u_pos[:, :, k] + utens[:, :, k] + utens_stage[:, :, k] + correction_term
        divided = 1.0 / bcol
        ccol[:, :, k] = ccol[:, :, k] * divided
        dcol[:, :, k] = dcol[:, :, k] * divided
    for k in range(1, K - 1):
        gav = -0.25 * (wcon[1:, :, k] + wcon[:-1, :, k])
        gcv = 0.25 * (wcon[1:, :, k + 1] + wcon[:-1, :, k + 1])
        as_ = gav * BET_M
        cs = gcv * BET_M
        acol = gav * BET_P
        ccol[:, :, k] = gcv * BET_P
        bcol = dtr_stage - acol - ccol[:, :, k]
        correction_term = -as_ * (u_stage[:, :, k - 1] - u_stage[:, :, k]) - cs * (u_stage[:, :, k + 1] - u_stage[:, :, k])
        dcol[:, :, k] = dtr_stage * u_pos[:, :, k] + utens[:, :, k] + utens_stage[:, :, k] + correction_term
        divided = 1.0 / (bcol - ccol[:, :, k - 1] * acol)
        ccol[:, :, k] = ccol[:, :, k] * divided
        dcol[:, :, k] = (dcol[:, :, k] - dcol[:, :, k - 1] * acol) * divided
    for k in range(K - 1, K):
        gav = -0.25 * (wcon[1:, :, k] + wcon[:-1, :, k])
        as_ = gav * BET_M
        acol = gav * BET_P
        bcol = dtr_stage - acol
        correction_term = -as_ * (u_stage[:, :, k - 1] - u_stage[:, :, k])
        dcol[:, :, k] = dtr_stage * u_pos[:, :, k] + utens[:, :, k] + utens_stage[:, :, k] + correction_term
        divided = 1.0 / (bcol - ccol[:, :, k - 1] * acol)
        dcol[:, :, k] = (dcol[:, :, k] - dcol[:, :, k - 1] * acol) * divided
    for k in range(K - 1, K - 2, -1):
        datacol = dcol[:, :, k]
        data_col[:] = datacol
        utens_stage[:, :, k] = dtr_stage * (datacol - u_pos[:, :, k])
    for k in range(K - 2, -1, -1):
        datacol = dcol[:, :, k] - ccol[:, :, k] * data_col[:, :]
        data_col[:] = datacol
        utens_stage[:, :, k] = dtr_stage * (datacol - u_pos[:, :, k])

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def sqr_dists(X: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], Y: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    return np.array([[np.sum((x - y) ** 2) for x in X] for y in Y])

@zk_circuit
def sqr_dists_loops(X: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], Y: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    m, n = (X.shape[0], Y.shape[0])
    D = np.zeros((m, n))
    for i in range(m):
        for j in range(n):
            D[i, j] = np.sum((X[i] - Y[j]) ** 2)
    return D

def dists(X: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], Y: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: data-dependent-loop, denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add an explicit iteration cap or classify as fail-closed until a bound is available.
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: while loops require a public/padded maximum iteration count.

@zk_chip
def swap(l: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], idx1: Float, idx2: Float):
    if idx1 != idx2:
        tmp = l[idx1]
        l[idx1] = l[idx2]
        l[idx2] = tmp

@zk_chip
def partition(l: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    size = len(l)
    pivot_idx = size // 2
    val = l[pivot_idx]
    idx = size - 1
    if pivot_idx != idx:
        swap(l, pivot_idx, idx)
    idx = idx - 1
    i = 0
    while i <= idx:
        if l[i] > val:
            while l[idx] > val and idx > i:
                idx = idx - 1
            if idx != i:
                swap(l, i, idx)
                idx = idx - 1
            else:
                break
        i = i + 1
    assert idx == i or idx + 1 == i
    swap(l, i, size - 1)
    return i

@zk_circuit
def QuickSort(l: Integer[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    size = len(l)
    if size > 1:
        idx = partition(l)
        l1 = []
        l2 = []
        for i in range(0, idx):
            l1.append(l[i])
        for i in range(idx, size):
            l2.append(l[i])
        QuickSort(l1)
        QuickSort(l2)
        for i in range(0, len(l1)):
            l[i] = l1[i]
        for i in range(0, len(l2)):
            l[len(l1) + i] = l2[i]
    return l

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.

@zk_chip
def call(i: Integer, j: Integer):
    return i + j

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda size: 0 <= size <= 1024)
def approximated_callgraph(size: Integer):
    out = list()
    for i in range(size):
        out.append(list(map(lambda j: call(i, j), range(size))))
    return out

# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def arc_distance(theta_1: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], phi_1: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], theta_2: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], phi_2: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    """
    Calculates the pairwise arc distance between all points in vector a and b.
    """
    temp = np.sin((theta_2 - theta_1) / 2) ** 2 + np.cos(theta_1) * np.cos(theta_2) * np.sin((phi_2 - phi_1) / 2) ** 2
    distance_matrix = 2 * np.arctan2(np.sqrt(temp), np.sqrt(1 - temp))
    return distance_matrix

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: data-dependent-loop, denominator-obligation
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add an explicit iteration cap or classify as fail-closed until a bound is available.
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Diagnostic: while loops require a public/padded maximum iteration count.

@zk_circuit
def is_square(a_positive_int: Float):
    """ Check if it is a perfect square. """
    x = a_positive_int // 2
    seen = {x}
    while x * x != a_positive_int:
        x = (x + a_positive_int // x) // 2
        if x in seen:
            return False
        seen.add(x)
    return True

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, domain-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import math

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda stock_price, option_strike, option_years, Riskfree, Volatility, nb_opt: Volatility != 0.0)
@requires(lambda stock_price, option_strike, option_years, Riskfree, Volatility, nb_opt: 0 <= nb_opt <= 1024)
def BlackScholes(stock_price: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], option_strike: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], option_years: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], Riskfree: Float, Volatility: Float, nb_opt: Integer):
    RSQRT2PI = 1 / math.sqrt(math.pi * 2)
    A1 = 0.31938153
    A2 = -0.356563782
    A3 = 1.781477937
    A4 = -1.821255978
    A5 = 1.330274429
    call_result = []
    put_result = []
    for opt in range(0, nb_opt):
        sqrtT = math.sqrt(option_years[opt])
        d1 = math.log(stock_price[opt] / option_strike[opt])
        d1 += (Riskfree + 0.5 * Volatility * Volatility) * option_years[opt]
        d1 /= Volatility * sqrtT
        d2 = d1 - Volatility * sqrtT
        K = 1.0 / (1.0 + 0.2316419 * abs(d1))
        CNDD1 = RSQRT2PI * math.exp(-0.5 * d1 * d1) * (K * (A1 + K * (A2 + K * (A3 + K * (A4 + K * A5)))))
        K = 1.0 / (1.0 + 0.2316419 * abs(d2))
        CNDD2 = RSQRT2PI * math.exp(-0.5 * d2 * d2) * (K * (A1 + K * (A2 + K * (A3 + K * (A4 + K * A5)))))
        expRT = math.exp(-Riskfree * option_years[opt])
        call_result.append(stock_price[opt] * CNDD1 - option_strike[opt] * expRT * CNDD2)
        put_result.append(option_strike[opt] * expRT * (1.0 - CNDD2) - stock_price[opt] * (1.0 - CNDD1))
    return (call_result, put_result)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.

@zk_circuit
def bubble_sort(list0: Integer[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    list1 = [x for x in list0]
    for i in range(0, len(list1) - 1):
        swap_test = False
        for j in range(0, len(list1) - i - 1):
            if list1[j] > list1[j + 1]:
                list1[j], list1[j + 1] = (list1[j + 1], list1[j])
            swap_test = True
        if swap_test == False:
            break
    return list1

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: domain-obligation, linalg
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Record square/invertible/SPD requirements where the linalg call needs them.
# Diagnostic: np.linalg kernels may stay fail-closed until support is selected.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def thin_plate_spline(r: Integer, xp: Float):
    return xp.where(r == 0, 0, r ** 2 * xp.log(r))

@zk_chip
def _kernel_matrix_impl(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], y: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], kernel_func: Float, xp: Float):
    """Evaluate RBFs, with centerx at `y`, at `x`."""
    return kernel_func(xp.linalg.norm(x[None, :, :] - y[:, None, :], axis=-1), xp)

@zk_circuit
def build_system(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024 K<=1024'], y: Float, powers: Float, xp: Float):
    """Construct the coefficients needed to evaluate
    the RBF.

    Parameters
    ----------
    x : (Q, N) float ndarray
        Evaluation point coordinates.
    y : (P, N) float ndarray
        Data point coordinates.
    powers : (R, N) int ndarray
        The exponents for each monomial in the polynomial.
    xp

    Returns
    -------
    (Q, P + R) float ndarray

    """
    kernel_func = thin_plate_spline
    vec = xp.concatenate([_kernel_matrix_impl(y, x, kernel_func, xp), xp.prod(x[:, None, :] ** powers, axis=-1)], axis=-1)
    return vec

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: data-dependent-loop, denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add an explicit iteration cap or classify as fail-closed until a bound is available.
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: while loops require a public/padded maximum iteration count.

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda t, t_stop, dt, dx, dy, u, um, k: dt != 0.0)
@requires(lambda t, t_stop, dt, dx, dy, u, um, k: dx != 0.0)
@requires(lambda t, t_stop, dt, dx, dy, u, um, k: dy != 0.0)
def timeloop(t: Float, t_stop: Float, dt: Float, dx: Float, dy: Float, u: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], um: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], k: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    while t <= t_stop:
        t += dt
        new_u = calculate_u(dt, dx, dy, u, um, k)
        um = u
        u = new_u
    return u

@zk_chip
def calculate_u(dt: Float, dx: Float, dy: Float, u: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], um: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], k: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    up = [[0.0] * len(u[0]) for i in range(len(u))]
    'omp parallel for'
    for i in range(1, len(u) - 1):
        for j in range(1, len(u[0]) - 1):
            up[i][j] = 2 * u[i][j] - um[i][j] + (dt / dx) ** 2 * (0.5 * (k[i + 1][j] + k[i][j]) * (u[i + 1][j] - u[i][j]) - 0.5 * (k[i][j] + k[i - 1][j]) * (u[i][j] - u[i - 1][j])) + (dt / dy) ** 2 * (0.5 * (k[i][j + 1] + k[i][j]) * (u[i][j + 1] - u[i][j]) - 0.5 * (k[i][j] + k[i][j - 1]) * (u[i][j] - u[i][j - 1]))
    return up

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: complex, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: Complex kernels require Complex dtype support or a fail-closed classification.

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda N, CA, CX, INCX, CY, INCY: 0 <= N <= 1024)
def CAXPY(N: Integer, CA: Complex, CX: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], INCX: Integer, CY: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], INCY: Integer):
    if N <= 0:
        return
    if abs(CA) == 0.0:
        return
    if INCX == 1 and INCY == 1:
        'omp parallel for'
        for I in range(N):
            CY[I] = CY[I] + CA * CX[I]
    else:
        IX = 0
        IY = 0
        if INCX < 0:
            IX = (-N + 1) * INCX
        if INCY < 0:
            IY = (-N + 1) * INCY
        for I in range(N):
            CY[IY] = CY[IY] + CA * CX[IX]
            IX = IX + INCX
            IY = IY + INCY
    return CY

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def check_mask(db: Bool[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], out: Bool[Array, 'N<=1024'], mask: Integer[Array, 'N<=1024']=(1, 0, 1)):
    for idx, line in enumerate(db):
        target, vector = (line[0], line[1:])
        if (mask == np.bitwise_and(mask, vector)).all():
            if target == 1:
                out[idx] = 1
    return out

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: complex, denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: Complex kernels require Complex dtype support or a fail-closed classification.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def limit1(x: Float, epsilon: Float=1e-06):
    if abs(x) < epsilon:
        return 0
    else:
        return x / abs(x)

@zk_circuit
def clip(z: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], _max: Float):
    out = np.empty(z.shape, dtype=z.dtype)
    for i in range(len(z)):
        if abs(z[i]) > _max:
            out[i] = limit1(z[i]) * _max
        else:
            out[i] = z[i]
    return out

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, domain-obligation, in-place-mutation
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def limit(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], epsilon: Float=1e-06):
    out = np.empty(shape=x.shape, dtype=x.dtype)
    mask1 = np.abs(x) < epsilon
    out[mask1] = 0
    mask2 = np.logical_not(mask1)
    out[mask2] = x[mask2] / np.abs(x[mask2])
    return out

@zk_circuit
def clip(z: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], _max: Float):
    mask = np.abs(z) > _max
    z[mask] = limit(z[mask]) * _max
    return z

@requires(lambda n_max: 0 <= n_max <= 1024)
def collatz(n_max: Integer):
    n = np.arange(3, n_max + 1)
    count = np.ones_like(n)
    indices = n != 1
    while np.any(indices):
        count[indices] += 1
        x = n[indices]
        n[indices] = np.where(x & 1, 3 * x + 1, x // 2)
        indices = n != 1
    max_i = np.argmax(count)
    return (max_i + 3, count[max_i])

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: data-dependent-loop, denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add an explicit iteration cap or classify as fail-closed until a bound is available.
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: while loops require a public/padded maximum iteration count.

@zk_circuit
def collatz_modified(target: Integer):
    start = 1
    while True:
        i = start
        steps = 0
        while True:
            if i == 1:
                break
            if i % 2 == 0:
                i = i // 2
            else:
                i = 3 * i + 1
            steps += 1
        if steps == target:
            return start
        start += 1

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, domain-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, mask-where
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Record mask shape equality and active-element bounds for where/nonzero-style indexing.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def compute_subpix_2d_gaussian2(correl: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], ix: Float, iy: Float):
    correl_crop = correl[iy - 1:iy + 2, ix - 1:ix + 2]
    tmp = np.where(correl_crop < 0)
    for i0, i1 in zip(tmp[0], tmp[1]):
        correl_crop[i0, i1] = 1e-06
    c10 = 0
    c01 = 0
    c11 = 0
    c20 = 0
    c02 = 0
    for i in range(1):
        for j in range(1):
            c10 += (i - 1) * np.log(correl_crop[j, i])
            c01 += (j - 1) * np.log(correl_crop[j, i])
            c11 += (i - 1) * (j - 1) * np.log(correl_crop[j, i])
            c20 += (3 * (i - 1) ** 2 - 2) * np.log(correl_crop[j, i])
            c02 += (3 * (j - 1) ** 2 - 2) * np.log(correl_crop[j, i])
            c00 = (5 - 3 * (i - 1) ** 2 - 3 * (j - 1) ** 2) * np.log(correl_crop[j, i])
    c00, c10, c01, c11, c20, c02 = (c00 / 9, c10 / 6, c01 / 6, c11 / 4, c20 / 6, c02 / 6)
    deplx = np.float32((c11 * c01 - 2 * c10 * c02) / (4 * c20 * c02 - c11 ** 2))
    deply = np.float32((c11 * c10 - 2 * c01 * c20) / (4 * c20 * c02 - c11 ** 2))
    return (deplx, deply, correl_crop)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def sigmoid(z: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    return 1 / (1 + np.exp(-z))

@zk_circuit
def convnet(conv_matrix: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], qcnn_filter: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], length: Integer, batch_size: Integer, state_size: Integer):
    state_size_2 = state_size * 2
    state_size_3 = state_size * 3
    state_size_4 = state_size * 4
    conv_results = np.dot(conv_matrix, qcnn_filter).reshape(length, batch_size, state_size_4)
    conv_results[:, :, :state_size] = np.tanh(conv_results[:, :, :state_size])
    conv_results[:, :, state_size:state_size_4] = sigmoid(conv_results[:, :, state_size:state_size_4])
    state_results = np.zeros((batch_size, length, state_size_2), dtype=np.float32)
    state = np.zeros((batch_size, state_size), dtype=np.float32)
    for i in range(length):
        z = conv_results[i, :, :state_size]
        f = conv_results[i, :, state_size:state_size_2]
        o = conv_results[i, :, state_size_2:state_size_3]
        state = f * state + (1 - f) * z
        state_results[:, i, :state_size] = state * o
    state = np.zeros((batch_size, state_size), dtype=np.float32)
    for i in range(length - 1, -1, -1):
        z = conv_results[i, :, :state_size]
        f = conv_results[i, :, state_size_3:state_size_4]
        o = conv_results[i, :, state_size_2:state_size_3]
        state = f * state + (1 - f) * z
        state_results[:, i, state_size:] = state * o
    state_results = state_results.reshape((batch_size * length, state_size_2))
    return state_results

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.

@zk_circuit
def cronbach(itemscores: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    itemvars = itemscores.var(1, None, None, 1)
    tscores = itemscores.sum(0)
    nitems = len(itemscores)
    return nitems / (nitems - 1) * (1 - itemvars.sum() / tscores.var(None, None, None, 1))

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: complex, denominator-obligation, domain-obligation
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Add operation-specific domains: sqrt arg >= 0, log arg > 0, acos arg in [-1, 1].
# Diagnostic: Complex kernels require Complex dtype support or a fail-closed classification.
import math

@zk_circuit
def CROTG(CA: Complex, CB: Complex, C: Float=0, S: Complex=0):
    if abs(CA) == 0.0:
        C = 0.0
        S = complex(1.0, 0.0)
        CA = CB
    else:
        SCALE = abs(CA) + abs(CB)
        NORM = SCALE * math.sqrt(abs(CA / SCALE) ** 2 + abs(CB / SCALE) ** 2)
        ALPHA = CA / abs(CA)
        C = abs(CA) / NORM
        S = ALPHA * CB.conjugate() / NORM
        CA = ALPHA * NORM
    return (CA, CB, C, S)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: complex, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: diagnostic/fail-closed candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
# Diagnostic: Complex kernels require Complex dtype support or a fail-closed classification.

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda n, sig, alp, dg, dh1, dh3, bin, nu: 0 <= n <= 1024)
def deriv(n: Integer, sig: Float, alp: Complex, dg: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], dh1: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], dh3: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024'], bin: Complex[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], nu: Float):
    dh2 = [complex(0, 0) for _ in dh1]
    ci = complex(0.0, 1.0)
    dh1[0] = complex(0.5, 0) * ci * complex(sig, 0)
    exp1 = complex(-0.5, 0)
    dh2[0] = alp
    exp2 = complex(-1.0, 0)
    dh3[0] = -2.0 * nu
    exp3 = complex(-1.0, 0)
    for i in range(1, n):
        dh1[i] = dh1[i - 1] * exp1
        exp1 = exp1 - 1.0
        dh2[i] = dh2[i - 1] * exp2
        exp2 = exp2 - 1.0
        dh3[i] = -nu * dh3[i - 1] * exp3
        exp3 = exp3 - 1.0
    dg[0] = complex(1.0)
    dg[1] = dh1[0] + dh2[0] + dh3[0]
    for i in range(2, n + 1):
        dg[i] = dh1[i - 1] + dh2[i - 1] + dh3[i - 1]
        for j in range(1, i):
            dg[i] = dg[i] + bin[j - 1][i - 1] * (dh1[j - 1] + dh2[j - 1] + dh3[j - 1]) * dg[i - j]
    return dg

np_descent(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], d: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], mu: Float, N_epochs: Integer)

Preconditions

  • Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at the exact division/modulo sites.
  • Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly, e.g. A[N,K] with x[K].
  • Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.

0 ok / 1 errors / 0 timeouts across 1 entry circuit(s)

Source excerpt 
from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded, matmul-dot
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Declare shared matrix/vector dimensions explicitly; when not expressible as @requires, encode with matching symbolic annotation names and keep as review obligation.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import itertools as it
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
@requires(lambda x, d, mu, N_epochs: 0 <= N_epochs <= 1024)
def np_descent(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], d: Float[Array, 'N<=1024'], mu: Float, N_epochs: Integer):
    N = len(x)
    f = 2 / N
    y = np.zeros(N)
    err = np.zeros(N)
    w = np.zeros(2)
    grad = np.empty(2)
    for _ in it.repeat(None, N_epochs):
        err[:] = d - y
        grad[:] = (f * np.sum(err), f * np.dot(err, x))
        w += mu * grad
        y = w[0] + w[1] * x
    return w

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_chip
def clamp(i: Integer, offset: Float, maxval: Float):
    j = max(0, i + offset)
    return min(j, maxval)

@zk_circuit
def reflect(pos: Float, offset: Float, bound: Float):
    idx = pos + offset
    return min(2 * (bound - 1) - idx, max(idx, -idx))

@zk_circuit
def conv(x: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], weights: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024']):
    sx = x.shape
    sw = weights.shape
    result = np.zeros_like(x)
    for i in range(sx[0]):
        for j in range(sx[1]):
            for ii in range(sw[0]):
                for jj in range(sw[1]):
                    idx = (clamp(i, ii - sw[0] // 2, sw[0]), clamp(j, jj - sw[0] // 2, sw[0]))
                    result[i, j] += x[idx] * weights[ii, jj]
    return result

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.
import numpy as np

@zk_circuit
def diffuseNumpy(u: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], tempU: Float[Array, 'N<=1024 M<=1024'], iterNum: Float):
    """
    Apply Numpy matrix for the Forward-Euler Approximation
    """
    mu = 0.1
    for n in range(iterNum):
        tempU[1:-1, 1:-1] = u[1:-1, 1:-1] + mu * (u[2:, 1:-1] - 2 * u[1:-1, 1:-1] + u[0:-2, 1:-1] + u[1:-1, 2:] - 2 * u[1:-1, 1:-1] + u[1:-1, 0:-2])
        u[:, :] = tempU[:, :]
        tempU[:, :] = 0.0

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: shape-dtype-only
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Shape/rank/dtype annotations only.

@zk_circuit
def Eandg(rOH: Float, thetaHOH: Float):
    """"
    Arguments: (internal coordinates of the water molecule)

    rOH            O-H bond distance
    thetaHOH       H-O-H bond angle


    Returns:

    E              Molecular force field energy
    grOH           O-H bond stretch gradient
    grthetaHOH     H-O-H bond angle bend gradient 

    
    Force field parameters:

    kOH            Harmonic force constant, O-H bond strech
    rOHe           Equilibrium distance, O-H
    kHOH           Harmonic angle bend force constant, H-O-H angle bend
    thetaHOHe      Equilibrium angle, H-O-H

    """
    kOH = 50.0
    rOHe = 0.95
    kHOH = 50.0
    thetaHOHe = 104.5
    E = 2 * kOH * (rOH - rOHe) ** 2 + kHOH * (thetaHOH - thetaHOHe) ** 2
    grOH = 2 * kOH * (rOH - rOHe)
    grthetaHOH = 2 * kHOH * (thetaHOH - thetaHOHe)
    return (E, grOH, grthetaHOH)

from zinnia import *
# Zinnia benchmark contract draft: generated for review.
# Keep kernel body faithful; refine TODO contracts before final benchmark runs.
# Categories: denominator-obligation, in-place-mutation, loop-bounded
# Expected outcome: compile/prove candidate
# Precondition: Add nonzero or positive denominator facts at exact division/modulo sites when expressible.
# Precondition: Loop trip counts must be finite and tied to scalar bounds or array dimensions.
# Precondition: Use writable fixed/bounded array shapes and record an aliasing policy for mutated inputs.

@zk_chip
def next_num(num: Integer):
    if num & 1:
        return 3 * num + 1
    else:
        return num // 2

@zk_chip
def series_length(num: Integer, lengths: Float[Array, 'N<=1024']):
    if num in lengths:
        return lengths[num]
    else:
        num2 = next_num(num)
        result = 1 + series_length(num2, lengths)
        lengths[num] = result
        return result

@zk_circuit
def euler14(MAX_NUM: Integer):
    num_with_max_length = 1
    max_length = 0
    lengths = {1: 0}
    for ii in range(1, MAX_NUM):
        length = series_length(ii, lengths)
        if length > max_length:
            max_length = length
            num_with_max_length = ii
    return (num_with_max_length, max_length)
